**[31:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4VFLczBURvY&t=1900s)** So if the wife was to have a child, the child would take her name and not the father's name. and that's basically what li'an entails and so what we've seen is that it is a punishment sorry, a means to a punishment potentially in the Akhirah but the believer, obviously we try and do our best to stay away from Zina but if someone was to be in this situation, well there are other believers you can still show them compassion, empathy empathy and mercy and they can still show it to you so Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala said that if anyone the dignity of these people is preserved so let's stop there Allah bless you all and we'll continue from this point next time inshallah wasallallahu ala sayyidina muhammadin wa ala alihi wa sahbihi wa salam walhamdulillahi rabbil alameen
